L2247: ## **Comment:**
L2248: **YES** 32. The preferred presentation for cross-references in the FPI is the section (not subsection) heading followed by the numerical identifier. The entire cross-reference should be in *italics* and enclosed within brackets. For example, "[*see Warnings and Precautions (5.2)*]."
L2249: ## **Comment:**
L2250: **N/A**
L2251: ## Selected Requirements of Prescribing Information
L2252: 33. For each RMC listed in HL, the corresponding new or modified text in the FPI must be marked with a vertical line on the left edge.
L2253: ## **Comment:**
L2254: ## FULL PRESCRIBING INFORMATION DETAILS
L2255: ## FPI Heading
L2256: **YES** 34. The following heading “**FULL PRESCRIBING INFORMATION**” must be **bolded**, must appear at the beginning of the FPI, and should be in UPPER CASE.
L2257: ## **Comment:**
L2258: ## BOXED WARNING Section in the FPI
L2259: **N/A** 35. All text in the BW should be **bolded**.
L2260: ## **Comment:**
L2261: **N/A** 36. The BW must have a title in UPPER CASE, following the word “**WARNING**” and other words to identify the subject of the warning. (Even if there is more than one warning, the term, “**WARNING**” and not “**WARNINGS**” should be used.) For example: “**WARNING: SERIOUS INFECTIONS and ACUTE HEPATIC FAILURE**”. If there is more than one warning in the BW title, the word “and” in lower case can separate the warnings.
L2262: ## **Comment:**
L2263: ## CONTRAINDICATIONS Section in the FPI
L2264: **N/A** 37. If no Contraindications are known, this section must state “None.”
L2265: ## **Comment:**
L2266: ## ADVERSE REACTIONS Section in the FPI
L2267: **YES** 38. When clinical trials adverse reactions data are included (typically in the “Clinical Trials Experience” subsection), the following verbatim statement (or appropriate modification) should precede the presentation of adverse reactions from clinical trials:
L2268: “Because clinical trials are conducted under widely varying conditions, adverse reaction rates observed in the clinical trials of a drug cannot be directly compared to rates in the clinical trials of another drug and may not reflect the rates observed in practice.”
L2269: ## **Comment:**
L2270: **N/A** 39. When postmarketing adverse reaction data are included (typically in the “Postmarketing Experience” subsection), the following verbatim statement (or appropriate modification) should precede the presentation of adverse reactions:
L2271: “The following adverse reactions have been identified during post-approval use of (insert drug name). Because these reactions are reported voluntarily from a population of uncertain size, it is not always possible to reliably estimate their frequency or establish a causal relationship to drug exposure.”
L2272: ## **Comment:**
L2273: ## Selected Requirements of Prescribing Information
L2274: ## PATIENT COUNSELING INFORMATION Section in the FPI
L2275: **YES** 40. Must reference any FDA-approved patient labeling in Section 17 (PATIENT COUNSELING INFORMATION). The reference statement should appear at the beginning of Section 17 and include the type(s) of FDA-approved patient labeling (e.g., Patient Information, Instructions for Use, or Medication Guide). Recommended language for the reference statement should include one of the following five verbatim statements that is most applicable:

- Advise the patient to read the FDA-approved patient labeling (Patient Information).
- Advise the patient to read the FDA-approved patient labeling (Instructions for Use).
- Advise the patient to read the FDA-approved patient labeling (Patient Information and Instructions for Use).
- Advise the patient to read the FDA-approved patient labeling (Medication Guide).
- Advise the patient to read the FDA-approved patient labeling (Medication Guide and Instructions for Use).
**Comment:**

**YES** 41. FDA-approved patient labeling (e.g., Patient Information, Instructions for Use, or Medication Guide) must not be included as a subsection under Section 17 (PATIENT COUNSELING INFORMATION). All FDA-approved patient labeling must appear at the end of the PI upon approval.

L2341: Figure 2: Cytokine Targeting of Biologics for Plaque Psoriasis. Figure adopted and reconstructed from literature Nature Biotech, 2011(29): 614-625.
This diagram illustrates the cytokine network in plaque psoriasis and the biologics targeting specific cytokines.
TNF-\alpha: Targeted by Etanercept (2004), Infliximab (2006), and Adalimumab (2008).
IL-12/23p40: Targeted by Ustekinumab (2009).
IL-23: Targeted by Guselkumab (Phase 3) and Tildrakizumab (Phase 3).
IL-17A: Targeted by Secukinumab (2015) and Ixekizumab (2016).
IL-17RA: Targeted by Brodalumab.
Note: Dates reflect approval of psoriasis indication and are not indicative of the date of original approval of biologic.

L2343: Rates of Serious Infections in Psoriasis Trials per 100 Person-Yrs (95% CI)
Forest plot showing the rates of serious infections per 100 person-years (95% CI) for various psoriasis treatments. The Y-axis ranges from 0.0 to 3.0. The X-axis lists the treatments: Brodalumab, Adalimumab, Apremilast, Briakinumab, Etanercept, Infliximab, Ixekizumab, Secukinumab, and Ustekinumab. The rates are: Brodalumab (1.4, 95% CI 1.0-1.8), Adalimumab (1.7, 95% CI 1.3-2.1), Apremilast (1.6, 95% CI 0.9-2.3), Briakinumab (1.2, 95% CI 0.6-1.8), Etanercept (1.9, 95% CI 1.3-2.5), Infliximab (1.8, 95% CI 1.2-2.4), Ixekizumab (1.7, 95% CI 1.3-2.1), Secukinumab (1.5, 95% CI 1.0-2.0), and Ustekinumab (1.4, 95% CI 0.9-1.9).

L2347: STUDY DESIGN AND TREATMENT SCHEMA
The figure illustrates the study design and treatment schema for Study 2012-0104, spanning from Day 1 to Week 266. The study is divided into four phases: Screening (\ge 7 days, \le 30 days), Induction, Maintenance, and Long-term extension.
At Day 1, subjects are randomized (R) into four groups (2:2:1:1 ratio):
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
ustekinumab
placebo
The Induction phase runs from Day 1 to Week 12. The Maintenance phase runs from Week 12 to Week 52. The Long-term extension phase runs from Week 52 to Week 266.
Treatment assignments during the Maintenance and Long-term extension phases are:
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q4W brodalumab
140 mg Q8W brodalumab
The placebo group continues receiving placebo throughout the Maintenance and Long-term extension phases.
L2348: Study Design and Treatment Schema
Timeline: Day 1, Week 12, Week 52, Week 266.
Phases: Screening (\ge 7 days, \le 30 days), Induction, Withdrawal and retreatment with return of disease*, Long-term extension.
Randomization 1 (R 1:1:1) occurs at Day 1, leading to three arms:
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
Placebo
At Week 12, patients in the 210 mg Q2W brodalumab arm are re-randomized (R 1:1) based on sPGA:
If sPGA \ge 2, 210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
Placebo
At Week 12, patients in the 140 mg Q2W brodalumab arm are re-randomized (R 1:1) based on sPGA:
If sPGA \ge 2, 210 mg Q2W brodalumab
Placebo
The Placebo arm continues as 210 mg Q2W brodalumab until Week 52, then continues until Week 266.
L2349: Study Design and Treatment Schema
Timeline: Day 1, Week 12, Week 52, Week 266.
Phases: Screening (\ge 7 days, \le 30 days), Induction, Maintenance, Long-term extension.
Randomization 1 (R 2:2:1:1) occurs at Day 1, leading to four arms:
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
ustekinumab
placebo
At Week 12, patients in the 210 mg Q2W brodalumab arm are re-randomized (R 2:2:2:1) based on sPGA:
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q4W brodalumab
140 mg Q8W brodalumab
The ustekinumab arm continues until Week 52, then continues until Week 266.
The placebo arm continues as 210 mg Q2W brodalumab until Week 52, then continues until Week 266.
L2350: STUDY DESIGN AND TREATMENT SCHEMA
The figure illustrates the study design and treatment schema for Study 2012-0104, spanning from Day 1 to Week 266. The study is divided into four phases: Screening (\ge 7 days, \le 30 days), Induction, Maintenance, and Long-term extension.
At Day 1, subjects are randomized (R) into four groups (2:2:1:1 ratio):
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
ustekinumab
placebo
The Induction phase runs from Day 1 to Week 12. During this phase, the placebo group receives placebo, while the three active treatment groups receive their respective doses of brodalumab or ustekinumab.
The Maintenance phase runs from Week 12 to Week 52. During this phase, the placebo group receives placebo. The three active treatment groups are randomized (R) into three groups (2:2:1 ratio):
210 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q2W brodalumab
140 mg Q4W brodalumab
The Long-term extension phase runs from Week 52 to Week 266. During this phase, the placebo group receives placebo. The three active treatment groups receive 210 mg Q2W brodalumab.
L2351: Bar chart titled "Suicidal ideation and behavior in brodalumab psoriasis trials: Rate per 100 person-yrs by past psychiatric history". The Y-axis represents the Rate per 100 person-yrs, ranging from 0.0 to 4.0. The X-axis shows four categories of past psychiatric history: Depression, No depression, Suicidality, and No suicidality. The rates are approximately: Depression (~1.4), No depression (~0.2), Suicidality (~3.6), and No suicidality (~0.1).
